    <string name="fh_how_to_play_android">• Fruit Hunt is a game where the player can launch a knife in the direction of the fruits that fly above the direction of the knife.\n\n• Players can place their stake by selecting relevant chips from the bottom right corner of the screen or go with the default chip selected.\n&lt;image&gt;https://s.sporty.net/ng/main/res/866065d1bb20d82deee939d93654790a.png&lt;/image&gt;\n• Once the chip selection is finalised, the player can align the knife in any of the three different directions by tapping on the position markings provided.\n&lt;image&gt;https://s.sporty.net/ng/main/res/a6e5f6c0f7379d23ba3ed68f1d9685fc.png&lt;/image&gt;\n• Based on the position selected, player can either tap on the knife area or on the dotted line to launch it in the direction selected.\n\n• The fruit which the knife slashes first would reveal the outcome of the round.\n\n• There would be a multiplier hidden in the fruit that is revealed after the fruit is slashed.\n&lt;image&gt;https://s.sporty.net/ng/main/res/9b603a331457b9d7c03baa818a6d202e.png&lt;/image&gt;\n• Based on the multiplier revealed, the user would win x times the stake. For example, If the multiplier is 2x, the player would receive 2x of their stake value as the winning.\n\n• If any fruit is Rotten, it means the player has lost the bet.\n&lt;image&gt;https://s.sporty.net/ng/main/res/f7b6be026479f24ea4fa40314f81941e.png&lt;/image&gt;\n• If a player hasn’t switched on Fixed Coeff toggle switch in side menu, any Fruit can have 1.2x or 1.5x or 2x or 3x or 5x or Half Rotten (0.5x) or Rotten (0x) coefficient hidden inside.\n\n• If any fruit is Half Rotten, it means the player will be awarded with 0.5x of the stake amount. Half Rotten won’t appear if Fixed Coeff toggle is switched on.\n&lt;image&gt;https://s.sporty.net/ng/main/res/feb8050cf2acb1e70593f706c4737b92.png&lt;/image&gt;\n• If a player has switched on Fixed Coeff toggle switch in side menu, any Fruit can have 2x or Rotten (0x) coefficient hidden inside.\n\n• If a player misses slashing any fruit with knife, there wouldn’t be any deduction of wallet balance.\n\n• Players can control the sound and music by toggling them inside the side menu.\n\n• Players can visit Bet History page through the menu to access the previous rounds\' bet history.\n\n• Players who have relevant Free Gifts available for the game would see the Free gift icon on the bottom left corner of the screen.\n&lt;image&gt;https://s.sporty.net/ng/main/res/f68d16e359aa9f3f5d058a4110dfe289.png&lt;/image&gt;\n• Players can click on the chat icon and engage with other players of the game.\n\n• In case of slow internet, please expect delays in cashout and other features. You may reach out to support for any complaints/feedback.\n\n• Please note that this game can be played by a player in one device at a time only.</string>

    <string name="how_to_play1">1. The objective of this game is to choose the color of the drawn card, which can be either RED, BLACK, or a GREEN (see #4).\n\n2. Each round of this game consists of up to a maximum of FIVE playable turns (hands). In other words, the player can bet on the color of the next face-down card drawn from the deck for a maximum of FIVE times in a particular round.\n\n3. Please note that each round is played using a single, well-shuffled deck of 52 cards plus jokers (see #4). The player has the advantage of knowing the history of the previously drawn cards and can use this to their benefit when placing a bet on a decision.\n\n4. Caution: Watch out for the Green Joker cards shown below. These cards are rare but can appear randomly throughout the game. These cards are over and above the standard deck of 52 cards and are neither Red nor Black. Drawing this card will result in a loss of the current turn for the player.\n</string>
    <string name="how_to_play1_even">1. The objective of this game is to predict the total of three dice, which can either be EVEN or ODD.\n\n2. The game is played with three dice. Once the player places his/her bet, the dice are drawn one after the other. The values that appear on the dice are added to calculate the Total.\n\n3. Players can select their desired stake amount by adjusting the slider between the minimum and maximum amount or by pressing the chips.\n\n4. Each winning turn pays DOUBLE (2X).\n\n5.     <string name="sg_rush_text_how_to_play">Rush is a tren-ball crash game. The game involves a car moving continuously in a city landscape before leaving. The game play is simple as follow:\n\n• Player selects a Target Coefficient that they think will be achieved by the Car.\n\n• Player then selects their Bet Amount.\n\n• Player can place their bet manually by clicking on the “Place Bet” button or click on “Auto” button to enable automatic bets. The Target Coefficient and the Bet Amount set when the Auto Bet is turned on will be considered for all Auto Bets until the player stops the Auto Bet.\n\n• Player places the Bet (Manual or Auto).\n\n• As soon as the bet is placed, the car will speed up to leave. The House Coefficient will start once the car speeds up and will stop once the car leaves, which will be the exit/final house coefficient.\n\n• If the exit/final house coefficient is higher than or equal to the target coefficient selected by the player, the player wins. Cash-out = Bet Amount x Target Coefficient selected by the player.\n\n• The Winning Chance shown below target coefficient is an approximate value generated dynamically.\n\nHOUSE COEFFICIENT :\n\n• The winning House Coefficient starts at 1.01x and keeps on increasing as the car speeds up and moves ahead.\n\n• Your winnings are calculated as : “Your Bet x Target Coefficient set by you”.\n\n• Before the start of each round, our random number generator generates the exit/final house coefficient at which the car would leave.\n\nRANDOMIZATION :\n\n• The house coefficient in each round is generated by randomly by an algorithm.\n\nWINNING CHANCE :\n\n• Winning chance is calculated taking the RTP (Return To Player) into consideration based on cumulative probability calculations.</string>
